# Agents · S · part 28

1,248 entries, spp-pr-reviewer to squad7-telemetry-webgpu-ai-agent-53. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **spp-pr-reviewer**: Fresh-context reviewer for spp pull requests. Reads the PR diff cold, runs the quality gates (ruff / mypy / pytest), checks the git-flow and CHANGELOG conventions, and verifies the locked methodology invariants are intact. Invoke when a PR is ready to review and pass the PR number (e.g. "review PR 90"). Returns an explicit APPROVE / REQUEST CHANGES verdict and posts it as a PR review comment. ([JayLBean/supervised-prompt-producer](https://github.com/JayLBean/supervised-prompt-producer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spp-pr-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **sprakgranskare**: OBLIGATORISK svensk språkgranskning av Mer eller Mindre-kort. Putsar fråga + differensfråga till naturlig, korrekt svenska (grammatik, ordföljd, flyt, komparativ). Rör ALDRIG värden, enhet eller riktning. Körs på VARJE batch EFTER faktagranskaren, före kurator. "테스트 작성", "테스트 추가", "커버리지 보강" 같은 요청에 위임한다. (기능을 TDD로 새로 만드는 경우는 spring-hexagonal-tdd-implementer를 사용한다.) Spotless(spotlessCheck)로 포매팅을 결정적으로 검사하고, 포매터가 잡지 못하는 명명·구조 컨벤션은 스타일 가이드 기준으로 리뷰하는 read-only 검사기다. "스타일 체크", "린트 검사", "컨벤션 확인", "포매팅 검사" 같은 요청에 위임한다. (프로젝트 아키텍처 rules 위반 검토는 spring-code-reviewer 또는 spring-hexagonal-code-reviewer 중 저장소에 남아 있는 쪽이 담당한다.) "기능 구현", "TDD로 만들어줘", "이 API 구현", "버그 재현 후 수정" 같은 요청에 위임한다. ([whuk/claude-code-base](https://github.com/whuk/claude-code-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spring-tdd-implementer.md))

Use para /squad:ship. ([lchampz/squad](https://github.com/lchampz/squad/blob/HEAD/plugins/squad/agents/squad-delivery-manager.md))

- **squad-designer**: Dono da experiencia de usuario da squad — fluxo, layout, hierarquia visual, design system e acessibilidade. Traduz regra de negocio em algo que o usuario entende sem manual. - **squad-dev**: Squad Dev. Implements the approved squad plan in production code until the Tester's tests and the full suite are green and the Coverage gate passes, and fixes blocking review findings. Formats its own files; edits tests only to adapt call sites mechanically to its own signature change; no Git write operations. ([LarsLaskowski/Censio](https://github.com/LarsLaskowski/Censio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/squad-dev.md) · ★1 · 5 copies)
- **squad-dev**: Desenvolvedor senior da squad. Investiga antes de codar, critica especificacao ambigua, implementa card e RFC seguindo as convencoes vigentes do projeto, e nunca reporta sucesso sem evidencia. Use para /squad:build, e sempre que um card entra em developing. ([lchampz/squad](https://github.com/lchampz/squad/blob/HEAD/plugins/squad/agents/squad-dev.md))
- **squad-devils-advocate**: Squad Devil's Advocate. Challenges a feature's spec once before it is planned (missing scenarios and edge cases, criteria that would pass a wrong build, wrong answers to open questions), and a squad plan once before any code is written (tiers standard and security): checks assumptions against the code, looks for a simpler option, a wrong scope or a missed no-change outcome, and returns objections ranked major or minor with evidence. Read-only, no veto: minor objections become notes of the challenged document, the Lead answers major ones and decides. ([LarsLaskowski/Censio](https://github.com/LarsLaskowski/Censio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/squad-devils-advocate.md) · ★1 · 5 copies)

- **squad-security**: Modela ameaca, audita dependencia e segredo, e e o gate de seguranca antes de release. - **squad-tech-writer**: Escreve documentacao voltada a quem consome o projeto de fora — README, changelog, docs de API, guia de onboarding. Ativado quando o projeto tem consumidor externo (API publica, biblioteca, CLI distribuida). Use no gate de release e quando a superficie publica muda. ([lchampz/squad](https://github.com/lchampz/squad/blob/HEAD/plugins/squad/agents/squad-tech-writer.md))
- **squad-tester**: Squad Tester. Writes unit tests first from the acceptance criteria in the squad plan/spec, confirms they fail on the current code, and after implementation adds tests until the Coverage gate passes. Does not change production code. ([LarsLaskowski/Censio](https://github.com/LarsLaskowski/Censio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/squad-tester.md) · ★1 · 5 copies)
- **squad-tester**: Tester del squad. Ejecuta las comprobaciones locales, prueba un parche temporal en el build staging antes del push y, tras el push, el build, los logs y los tests en Odoo.sh, y devuelve evidencias concretas (comando, resultado, SHA, número de tests). - **squad-writer**: Redactor del squad. Escribe textos para personas con el modelo más ligero: mensajes al cliente, resumen de la tarea, descripción del PR e informe final. Solo redacta; nunca envía nada. Usar al final de la tarea. ([hachecito/odoo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/hachecito/odoo-agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/odoo-squad/agents/squad-writer.md))
